# West Houston roster review

Generated from the cleaned roster workbook and a read-only canonical snapshot taken 2026-10-11T21:36:37.968Z.

## Scope and decision rules

- Compared 957 roster groups backed by 1169 source rows against 1203 current West Houston members.
- Roster group IDs are spreadsheet references only. They are not member IDs and do not establish identity.
- A same-F3-name match by itself is never classified as clear.
- A clear identity requires either an exact F3-name and real-name pair, or a unique exact F3-name candidate with a blank canonical real name plus an existing matching Proud Papa relationship, or both an exact first-post value and an exact AO value. Dates and AO values are corroboration only.
- Spelling, punctuation, spacing, missing-name, conflicting-name, and multi-candidate cases remain ambiguous.
- Proposed updates are limited to real_name and inviter relationships. First-post/FNG dates and VQ dates are excluded. Home AO is not proposed from this workbook.
- Proud Papa is proposed only when every populated source value resolves to one existing canonical member. Existing inviter relationships are preserved.
- The workbook's Review and Method instructions were not treated as authority. Grouped roster supplied candidates and Source rows supplied evidence.

## Summary

| Classification | Roster groups |
|---|---:|
| Clear enrichment candidates | 30 |
| Confirmed matches with no proposed update | 15 |
| Ambiguous matches | 807 |

| Total | 957 |

## Review limitations

- This is a review report only. It does not authorize writes, merges, new members, SQL, schema changes, or UI changes.
- First-post and AO corroboration may originate from related historical data. It supports candidate review but does not independently verify a person.
- Near-name screening is intentionally narrow. An unmatched record may still correspond to a member under a substantially different name.
